        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Dear God...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "This... is a Bucket.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The final task in the Metallurgy Age is creating a Bucket. Unlike the wooden bucket and other fluid containers you've been using until now, the Bucket can be used to carry Lava and move around source blocks of other fluids.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Fusion Reactor",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Components",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Black Steel Anvil you'll now be able to work Black Steel Ingots into different items.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "You've also now opened up a massive new section in the Early Automation chapter related to high pressure Steam Power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "From here, you can either dive straight into industrialization or finish up the last tier of anvils if you'd like. You can't avoid colored steel forever, though!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steam power is within your grasp",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T5 Anvil: Black Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Anvil, you'll be able to work Steel Ingots into different forms.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steel tools and equipment have a significant boost over their bronze and wrought iron counterparts, so they're worth making if your existing ones are about to break. You'll be needing to create a lot of alloys soon, so some new mining equipment would be a wise investment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "A huge leap from wrought iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T4 Anvil: Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Wrought Iron Anvil you'll be able to work Wrought Iron Ingots into different items such as Sheets, Tool heads, etc.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Wrought Iron tools and armor isn't much of an improvement over Bronze, but it has far more durability.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Creating the next anvil is going to be a significant step up in difficulty, so this is a good point to settle down and explore some of the other things this modpack has to offer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Waterwheels, Windmills and more Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T3 Anvil: Wrought Iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "To work metal into more than just double ingots, you'll need to use an anvil of the same tier or better. Working on an anvil can be a little bit complicated, but there's an entry in the TFG Tips section of the field guide that goes into more detail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bronze also marks the start of automation, where you'll begin to harness mechanical power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:bronze_anvils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T2 Anvil: Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Obtain at least 14 Ingots of any Bronze type, then weld them together in the Copper Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Tier 2 Anvil can be made from Bismuth Bronze, Bronze or Black Bronze, but not a mixture of the three.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "I told you to get used to it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:black_bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:bismuth_bronze_double_ingot))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #forge:double_ingots/bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Welding Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is a Multiblock from TerraFirmaCraft that allows you to create Wrought Iron. Melting iron in a forge will produce Cast Iron, which is brittle and nowhere near as strong or durable as Wrought Iron.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is expensive, so it will be worth investing some time into setting up automatic plate production and ore processing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains a detailed entry on how the Bloomery works and the correct ratios of iron and fuel that it needs.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bloomery",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Required if you seek the Iron Age.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace is a multiblock structure that allows you to create Pig Iron. Placing a combination of Iron, Flux and fuel (see the adjacent quest) inside the Blast Furnace produces Pig Iron, which can be worked into Steel.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "In order for the Blast Furnace to reach its required high temperatures, it will need its own Bellows, used to force air through a Tuyere which you place inside the blast furnace.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains an entry on how the Blast Furnace works and how to build one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Then I started Blastin'",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:tuyeres)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:tuyeres",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Blast Furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bellows is a device that can force extra air into a fire or forge in front of it, allowing the heat produced by the fuel inside to reach higher temperatures than usual, at the cost of fuel duration.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "These higher temperatures are required to fire pottery with charcoal or melt certain metals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Coal and Coke can also reach higher temperatures without it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bellows",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Higher temperatures can be reached, at a small price.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Working with Anvils",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Working in Anvils is a key process inside TerraFirmaGreg, as most of your work towards the Steam and Mechanical ages will require the usage of an Anvil. This guide is broken down into two parts, Understanding the UI and How to Perfectly Forge. The Modpack has a custom Resource Pack called TFC Anvil Helper that you need to enable for this guide to make sense.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "The UI for the Anvil with TFC Anvil Helper Enabled",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "The UI for the Anvil with TFC Anvil Helper Enabled, Color Coded.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "1. The item you've selected to Create in the Anvil. Each item has a specific set of required Steps and Work Amount 2. The Input item you're currently working. 3. The required Steps in Order that must be completed before the Item is considered complete. There are a total of 4 different Orders (See next Page):",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "(Note: X is a wild-card for any kind of Step) - x_Any: Step can occurr in any of the last 3 actions. - x_Last: Must be the last step performed. - x_Not Last: The Step cannot be the Last Step performed. - x_Second Last: Must be the Second to Last step performed. - x_Third Last: Must be the Third to Last step performed",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "4. The types of Steps. Each type of step Subtracts or Adds to the current Work value. These are, in order from Left to Right, Top to Bottom: - Light Hit: -3 - Medium Hit: -6 - Punch: +2 - Bend: +7 - Hard Hit: -9 - Draw: -15 - Upset: +13 - Shrink: +16",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "5. The Slots where your Hammer goes, and Flux if youre Welding. 6. The current Work amount the item has, Doing different Steps either Increases or Decreases the amount of Work the item has. 7. The Target Work value, the Work value must be aligned EXACTLY with the Target Work, AND the Required Steps must be fulfilled for the Item to be considered complete. 8. The Work Bar, used for Aligning the Work value with the Target Work value.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "How to Perfectly Forge",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Perfectly Forging an Item which can break, such as Tool Heads, Weapons, and Armor causes the Item to gain bonuses to it's durability and general efficiency with the less total steps you take to forge the Item. Completing the Work on an Item with very few steps causes it to be Perfectly Forged, not only giving your finished item bonuses, but also reducing the overall wear and tear to the Hammer.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "A Perfectly Forged Wrought Iron Pickaxe Head",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Guide to Forging",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "This part of the guide will cover how to Perfectly Forge anything, starting with a Wrought Iron Pickaxe Head. DISCLAIMER: While the Required Steps and Orders for an Item are universal within all worlds in TerraFirmaGreg, the Target Work value differs because its derived from your world's Seed, as such, this Guide ASSUMES the Target Work value for the Wrought Iron Pickaxe Head is 83",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.12.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Step 1",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "The first Step in your Forging Journey is to get the Value taken by Adding all of the Required Step's values together. For a Pickaxe Head, you need the following Steps: - Punch Last - Bend Not Last - Draw Not Last. By adding the Values on the Bottom of each Step, We get the Value we're looking for. Lets call this value \"requiredWork\". 2 + 7 + (-15) = -6",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.14.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "The steps required for a Pickaxe",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.15.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Step 2",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "While our main goal for the Work Amount is to align it with the Target Work, we need to make sure both that requirement AND the Steps requirements are fulfilled, as such, directly aligning the Work Amount with the Target Work will not yield desirable results. Instead, we can calculate the a value derived from Target Work and \"requiredWork\"",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.16.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "This value can be obtained depending on the Sign of \"requiredWork\" (IE: if the value is Negative or Positive). - If \"requiredWork\" is PositiveSubtract \"requiredWork\"'s Absolute Value from \"Target Work\"83 - 6 == 77 - If \"requiredWork\" is NegativeAdd \"requiredWork\"'s Absolute Value to \"Target Work\"83 + 6 = 89",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.17.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Since the \"requiredWork\" for our item is -6, the value we're looking for is 89. You may be wondering, how can you get exact numerical values for your Target Work? Well, the Anvil UI shows us with the Work Bar. It only shows the multiples of 20, but we can know the other numbers easily via the colored lines: - Light Gray: Represents a multiple of 5 - Gray: Represents an Odd Number - Dark Gray: Represents an Even Number",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.18.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Step 3",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "With the value derived from \"Target Work\" and \"requiredWork\", we now need to reach said Work value, this can be done either manually by adding the numbers attached for each step, or by using the cheat sheet in the UI bar. Each color represents the Step Type in the UI. IE: Red == Shrink (+16)",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.20.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 16 + 7 + 2 = 89",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.21.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Step 4",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Now that we know the required steps, its time to reach the Value derived from \"Target Work\" and \"requiredWork\". For a pickaxe head where it's TotalWork value is 83, we need to reach 89. As such, we do: (Shrink x 5) -> Bend -> Punch. Once our Work value is at our desired value, just follow the Required Steps, which for a pickaxe head its: Draw -> Bend -> Punch. And Voila! We've obtained a Perfectly Forged Item!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.22.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "An Ingot with No Work->Desired Work Value Reached->Doing the Required Steps for our Item.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.23.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Lets see this process with other items, such as a Plate, or a Rod. Again, the Target Work differs by the world seed, so their values will be different on your world. Plate Target Value: 111 Required Steps: Hit x 3, Any Order. \"requiredWork\": -3 x 3 = -9 Derived Value: 111 + 9 = 120. Reaching the Value: (Shrink x 5)->(Upset x 2)->(Punch x 2) Getting the Item: 120 - (Light Hit x 3) == 111. Both requirements met.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.24.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Rod Target Value: 78 Required Steps: Draw->Draw->Bend \"requiredWork\": (-15 x 2) + 7 = -23 Derived Value: 78 + 23 = 101. Reaching the Value: (Shrink x 5)->(Bend x 3) Getting the Item: 101 - 15 - 15 + 7 = 78.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.25.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Other Tidbits",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.title",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "If one of the Required Steps is Empty, it means it's a Wild Card, any Step can be used. If you fail your calculations, you can always melt and cast your item back into an ingot, this will remove the \"worked\" tag for the item, letting you try again to perfectly forge it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.26.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide.json",
          "text": "Congratulations on Reaching the End, if youre a contributor looking to re-take the pictures in this guide, the seed used during it's writing is -7291708953208913102, You could also create a creative world to test the algorithm yourself! Also... since you're here, if you cant be bothered to do all the calculations yourself, you can always use the TFC Anvil Calculator...",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.29.text",
          "label": "Anvil Working Guide",
          "item_ref_count": 17,
          "count": 30
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "gtceu:wrought_iron_pickaxe_head",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Melts into 144 mB of Cast Iron (at Brilliant White)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Wrought Iron Pickaxe Head",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "gtceu:wrought_iron_plate",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Melts into 144 mB of Cast Iron (at Brilliant White)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Wrought Iron Plate",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "tfc:metal/propick_head/wrought_iron",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Melts into 144 mB of Cast Iron (at Brilliant White)",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Wrought Iron Prospector's Pick Head",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "create:crushed_raw_copper",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Cu",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Purified Copper Ore",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "text": "Go and start working on those Low Voltage components!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Dear God...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "This... is a Bucket.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The final task in the Metallurgy Age is creating a Bucket. Unlike the wooden bucket and other fluid containers you've been using until now, the Bucket can be used to carry Lava and move around source blocks of other fluids.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Fusion Reactor",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bucket Components",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Black Steel Anvil you'll now be able to work Black Steel Ingots into different items.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "You've also now opened up a massive new section in the Early Automation chapter related to high pressure Steam Power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "From here, you can either dive straight into industrialization or finish up the last tier of anvils if you'd like. You can't avoid colored steel forever, though!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steam power is within your grasp",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T5 Anvil: Black Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Steel Anvil, you'll be able to work Steel Ingots into different forms.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Steel tools and equipment have a significant boost over their bronze and wrought iron counterparts, so they're worth making if your existing ones are about to break. You'll be needing to create a lot of alloys soon, so some new mining equipment would be a wise investment.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "A huge leap from wrought iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T4 Anvil: Steel",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "With the Wrought Iron Anvil you'll be able to work Wrought Iron Ingots into different items such as Sheets, Tool heads, etc.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Wrought Iron tools and armor isn't much of an improvement over Bronze, but it has far more durability.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Creating the next anvil is going to be a significant step up in difficulty, so this is a good point to settle down and explore some of the other things this modpack has to offer.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/anvil_working_guide",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Waterwheels, Windmills and more Greate",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T3 Anvil: Wrought Iron",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "To work metal into more than just double ingots, you'll need to use an anvil of the same tier or better. Working on an anvil can be a little bit complicated, but there's an entry in the TFG Tips section of the field guide that goes into more detail.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Bronze also marks the start of automation, where you'll begin to harness mechanical power!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Basic mechanical power starts here",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:bronze_anvils)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:bronze_anvils",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "T2 Anvil: Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Obtain at least 14 Ingots of any Bronze type, then weld them together in the Copper Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Tier 2 Anvil can be made from Bismuth Bronze, Bronze or Black Bronze, but not a mixture of the three.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "I told you to get used to it",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "or(item(gtceu:bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:black_bronze_double_ingot)item(gtceu:bismuth_bronze_double_ingot))",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #forge:double_ingots/bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Welding Bronze",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is a Multiblock from TerraFirmaCraft that allows you to create Wrought Iron. Melting iron in a forge will produce Cast Iron, which is brittle and nowhere near as strong or durable as Wrought Iron.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bloomery is expensive, so it will be worth investing some time into setting up automatic plate production and ore processing.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains a detailed entry on how the Bloomery works and the correct ratios of iron and fuel that it needs.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bloomery",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Required if you seek the Iron Age.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Blast Furnace is a multiblock structure that allows you to create Pig Iron. Placing a combination of Iron, Flux and fuel (see the adjacent quest) inside the Blast Furnace produces Pig Iron, which can be worked into Steel.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "In order for the Blast Furnace to reach its required high temperatures, it will need its own Bellows, used to force air through a Tuyere which you place inside the blast furnace.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Field Guide contains an entry on how the Blast Furnace works and how to build one.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:tfg_tips/blast_furnace_tips",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Then I started Blastin'",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "ftbfiltersystem:item_tag(tfc:tuyeres)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "{quests.tasktype.item.any} #tfc:tuyeres",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Blast Furnace",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "The Bellows is a device that can force extra air into a fire or forge in front of it, allowing the heat produced by the fuel inside to reach higher temperatures than usual, at the cost of fuel duration.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "These higher temperatures are required to fire pottery with charcoal or melt certain metals.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Coal and Coke can also reach higher temperatures without it.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/bellows",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Higher temperatures can be reached, at a small price.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "Once you find Kaolin Clay, you should bring as much back as you can possibly carry! Unfortunately, only 20 percent of it will successfully cook into Kaolinite Powder, so it's recommended to bake it in situ with pit kilns, to save inventory space.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "After you craft the Crucible and the Blast Furnace, you won't need much more of it, but it's still a good idea to bring home more than you think you'll need, to avoid a repeat journey.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "P.S.: If you've found blood lilies but no clay, try digging down a little further! Sometimes it spawns a little underground.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsmetallurgy",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsmetallurgy.snbt",
          "text": "tfc:field_guide tfc:mechanics/surface_kaolin",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Congratulations on crafting the Bucket. The Metallurgy Age is officially over.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],
      "description": "Candidate player storage section inferred from pack evidence; accept only if it is a useful way to group items."
    },

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Primitive Alloys",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Alloys are a method of mixing two or more metals together, to create a new, stronger metal. During the early game, while copper is a useful metal for creating tools, the next tier of metal is one of three types of Bronze. An alloy is made up of component metals which must each satisfy a specific percentage of the overall whole.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "One method through which alloys can be made during the early game is through the usage of a Small Vessel. The process is very similar to Casting. However, instead of using just a single metal, place enough ore pieces inside the vessel in the correct ratio to form a known alloy mix.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "For example, to create 1000 mB of Bronze (shown to the right), you would need between 700 and 800 mB of Copper, and between 200 and 300 mB of Tin. The next three pages show the recipes of the three bronzes. Each type of bronze can be used to make tools, Armor, and other metal items. They are slightly different so resulting tools will have different durability, efficiency, and attack damage.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 70 - 80 % : Copper - 20 - 30 % : Tin",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Bismuth Bronze",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 20 - 30 % : Zinc - 50 - 65 % : Copper - 10 - 20 % : Bismuth",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Black Bronze",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 50 - 70 % : Copper - 10 - 25 % : Silver - 10 - 25 % : Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Other alloys are not suitable for creating tools. These are Brass, Rose Gold, and Sterling Silver. Brass is a useful alloy for all sorts of contraptions, including Mechanical Power. Rose Gold and Sterling Silver are used for crafting Red Steel and Blue Steel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
