        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Primitive Alloys",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Alloys are a method of mixing two or more metals together, to create a new, stronger metal. During the early game, while copper is a useful metal for creating tools, the next tier of metal is one of three types of Bronze. An alloy is made up of component metals which must each satisfy a specific percentage of the overall whole.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "One method through which alloys can be made during the early game is through the usage of a Small Vessel. The process is very similar to Casting. However, instead of using just a single metal, place enough ore pieces inside the vessel in the correct ratio to form a known alloy mix.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "For example, to create 1000 mB of Bronze (shown to the right), you would need between 700 and 800 mB of Copper, and between 200 and 300 mB of Tin. The next three pages show the recipes of the three bronzes. Each type of bronze can be used to make tools, Armor, and other metal items. They are slightly different so resulting tools will have different durability, efficiency, and attack damage.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 70 - 80 % : Copper - 20 - 30 % : Tin",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Bismuth Bronze",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 20 - 30 % : Zinc - 50 - 65 % : Copper - 10 - 20 % : Bismuth",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Black Bronze",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.title",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 50 - 70 % : Copper - 10 - 25 % : Silver - 10 - 25 % : Gold",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Other alloys are not suitable for creating tools. These are Brass, Rose Gold, and Sterling Silver. Brass is a useful alloy for all sorts of contraptions, including Mechanical Power. Rose Gold and Sterling Silver are used for crafting Red Steel and Blue Steel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "count": 10
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/primitive_alloys.json",
          "text": "Utility Alloys",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.title",
          "label": "Primitive Alloys",
          "item_ref_count": 9,

        },

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "A Crucible is an advanced device used for the creation of Alloys. It is a more precise method than using a Small Vessel to make alloys. To obtain a crucible, you will first need to obtain some Fire Clay, which is a stronger material than clay. This fire clay can then be knapped to shape it into an Unfired Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "Knapping an Unfired Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "After the crucible is knapped, it will need to be fired, like any piece of pottery - a Pit Kiln or Charcoal Forge would do. In order to use the crucible, it needs a source of heat. The crucible can be heated by any heatable block below, usually a Charcoal Forge",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "A crucible heated by a charcoal forge below it.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "Now, you are ready to use the crucible. When you use it, you will see the Crucible Interface, shown to the right. The top region shows the current metal content of the crucible. The first metal shown is what would be produced, if it were to be extracted right now. Other metals shown are the makeup of the current alloy in the crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "Advanced Alloying",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.title",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "The Crucible Interface",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "The crucible has nine slots where items can be added to be melted, and their liquefied contents will be directly added to the crucible. Molten metal containers such as Molds can also be placed here and they will be slowly drained into the crucible, allowing for precise control over your alloy's content. Holding while hovering over a draining mold or vessel makes it drain faster. Molds or other fluid containers can also be placed in the output slot, and will be slowly filled with the current content of the crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/crucible.json",
          "text": "The temperature indicator on the left will rise based on external sources of heat, such as a Charcoal Forge below or heat from a Blast Furnace from above. Metal can be extracted from the crucible as long as it is still molten. Finally, the crucible will keep its contents when broken, allowing you to transport the alloy container around if you wish.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Crucible",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 8
        },

        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "tfg:weak_blue_steel_dust",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Steel is an advanced material which can be used to create tools and Armor, and comes in a few different varieties: Steel, Black Steel, Red Steel, and Blue Steel. In order to create steel, you must first create Pig Iron in a Blast Furnace, and cast it into Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "A Pig Iron Ingot can then be worked in an anvil to create a High Carbon Steel ingot, which can be worked again to create a Steel Ingot",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Black Steel is an advanced form of steel formed from an alloy of steel and some other metals. In order to create it, you will need to create an alloy called Weak Steel in a Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Black Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.title",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Weak Steel",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.title",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 50 - 70 % : Steel - 15 - 25 % : Nickel - 15 - 25 % : Black Bronze Molten Weak Steel can be cast into ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Weak Steel Ingots can then be welded with Pig Iron Ingots to create High Carbon Black Steel Ingots. Finally, these can be worked on an Anvil to create Black Steel Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Black Steel can be used to craft tools and Armor, and also is used as a key ingredient in the creation of Blue Steel and Red Steel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Blue Steel is one of the two highest tier metals, along with Red Steel. Similar to Black Steel, the first step is to create an alloy of Weak Blue Steel in a Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },

          "text": "Weak Blue Steel Ingots can be welded with Black Steel Ingots to create High Carbon Blue Steel Ingots. Finally, these can be worked on an Anvil to create Blue Steel Ingots.",

        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Steel is an advanced material which can be used to create tools and Armor, and comes in a few different varieties: Steel, Black Steel, Red Steel, and Blue Steel. In order to create steel, you must first create Pig Iron in a Blast Furnace, and cast it into Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "A Pig Iron Ingot can then be worked in an anvil to create a High Carbon Steel ingot, which can be worked again to create a Steel Ingot",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Black Steel is an advanced form of steel formed from an alloy of steel and some other metals. In order to create it, you will need to create an alloy called Weak Steel in a Crucible.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Black Steel",

          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",

          "label": "Steel",

        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Requirements: - 50 - 70 % : Steel - 15 - 25 % : Nickel - 15 - 25 % : Black Bronze Molten Weak Steel can be cast into ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Weak Steel Ingots can then be welded with Pig Iron Ingots to create High Carbon Black Steel Ingots. Finally, these can be worked on an Anvil to create Black Steel Ingots.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,
          "recipe_ref_count": 4,
          "count": 14
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/mechanics/steel.json",
          "text": "Black Steel can be used to craft tools and Armor, and also is used as a key ingredient in the creation of Blue Steel and Red Steel.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Steel",
          "item_ref_count": 9,

          "kind": "guide_page",

          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_moon/lunarians.json",
          "text": "Throughout the moon, There are large, inhabited underground villages. These villages contain an alien form of the earth villager, who call themselves Lunarians. Unlike their earth counterpart, Lunarian don't need oxygen and can survive the chilling environment of the moon.",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge is a gratuitously large forge, with high capacity for fuel, products, and heating. It is constructed from Hellbricks, Anthracite, and Pyroxenite Aqueducts.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The Hellforge requires lava to flow in aqueducts in its four corners, as well as 21 hellbricks. The 3x3 center must be filled with Anthracite piles, which then may be lit to start the forge.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge.json",
          "text": "The item slots of the Hellforge may contain fuel, or things to be smelted; there is no distinction. Be careful what you put in! There are also slots for melting things, as with the charcoal forge. The lava meter on the screen indicates temperature (as well as the visual movement of its items in world).",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "The Hellforge",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "count": 4
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/beneath/hellforge",

          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Instead of crafting a wooden pickaxe, you can just pick up rocks from the ground. Begin by collecting at least 4 rocks. You can collect rocks by breaking or right-clicking them with an empty hand.",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",

          "text": "Find some Rocks",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Thirst is represented by the blue bar above your hunger. You can and will die of dehydration if you dont keep your thirst meter filled! The easiest way to have a drink is by drinking from natural ponds in the wild, by right-clicking on them with an empty hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "To complete this quest, you need to drink a Potable source of water, can be regular or river water. You may need to drink multiple times so the quest completes properly",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "You don't have to worry about purifying water here, though Salt Water will just make you more thirsty.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Remember to drink water!",

          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "To avoid having your inventory get clogged with different types of sticks, you can craft them into 'regular' sticks to stack them together. Later, you can bundle nine sticks into a Bunch, and the Bunch into a Bundle, which can then be used as a substitute for logs.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "You gotta handle that tool somehow",
          "key": "quest-snbt",

          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "While stone and flint are relatively simple and primitive materials, they're what you're stuck with until you learn how to work with metals. Some other useful tools include the Javelin, which can be thrown like a vanilla Trident, and the Hoe, which allows you to farm.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Turns out hitting stones together is quite useful",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "With your new Stone Axe, you'll be able to chop down trees. Breaking the base of a tree causes the entire tree to fall down, resulting in logs, sticks, and saplings.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Gather at least 10 logs -- 8 of these will be used for a Pit Kiln while the other two will be used for a Fire Pit.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",

        },
        {

          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",

        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Congratulations! With your Workbench you've officially entered the Peak of the Stone Age. With it you'll be able to craft a multitude of new things with the items you've gathered so far.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Now comes your main goal of the Primtive Age: proper metalworking with the Copper Anvil.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Sidenote: While you could gather enough materials to make 14 ingots of bronze right now, you won't be able to turn them into a bronze anvil without crafting the copper one first, sorry! At least anvils are recyclable, so you won't waste anything.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",

          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "To begin making your Saw, add at least 144mb of ore of your desired metal to a Vessel. Afterwards, place the Vessel in a Pit Kiln and light it on fire.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Once the Pit Kiln burns out, quickly pick up your vessel while it's still hot, while holding some Wooden Tongs in your offhand, and interact with it to open the Pouring interface. Simply place the Saw Mold in the Pouring Interface's slot and watch it fill with your very first Saw Head. Afterwards, combine your Saw Head with a Stick and you'll create your Saw!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "While the easiest metal to work with is Copper, you can try your hand at the process of alloying. This quest's tasks will accept either an item made out of Copper, or a Bronze Alloy.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Bismuth Bronze: Copper, Bismuth and Zinc. The weakest of the bronzes, but can be the easiest to make.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/questsstoneage",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Bronze: Copper and Tin. It's the most average of the bronzes.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Black Bronze: Copper, Gold, and Silver: The best bronze in the early game, but it's considerably more expensive.",

          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",

          "kind": "quest_node",

          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/questsstoneage.snbt",
          "text": "Relatively easy water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "As you begin your journey, the first thing you'll notice is a completely changed world. You can no longer break trees with your fists, but fret not, for any caveman is not a real caveman without his stone tools!",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },

          "text": "The Pit Kiln is an early game method of heating items up. We'll use it to both fire our clay items, and most importantly, melt very basic ores and create our first metal tool, a Saw.",

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "In TerraFirmaCraft, the first things you can obtain are sticks, twigs, and loose rocks. They can be found in almost every climate, lying scattered on the ground. or break these to pick them up.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "A smattering of common sticks and stones.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "In addition to gathering sticks and twigs on the ground, sticks can also be obtained by breaking leaves with your fist. Once you have a number of rocks and sticks, you are ready to start Knapping. Knapping is a process where two rocks are hit together, to form a particular shape. In order to knap, first hold at least two rocks in your hand, then right click in the air, which will open up the Knapping Interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "The Knapping Interface.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "In order to knap a particular item, you want to remove squares until you form the desired pattern. For example, create a knife blade by matching the recipe shown to the right. Like crafting recipes, the location of the desired pattern doesn't matter for the output, and some recipes have multiple variants that are valid.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "A knife blade, crafted from several different rock types.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "All stone tool heads can be crafted with a stick or twig to assemble them into a tool. The stone knife can be used to harvest Straw by breaking plants.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.6.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Axes can be used to cut down entire trees, logs and leaves included. However, saplings and sticks will only be obtained if leaves were broken individually.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.7.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Shovels can be used to dig soil type blocks. They can also be used to create Paths by using them on Dirt or Grass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.8.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Hoes are useful tools for Farming, but they can also be used to cut down leaves and other plant matter.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.9.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Hammers can be used as Crushing weapons, but are more important tools used for Forging.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.10.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/getting_started/introduction.json",
          "text": "Finally, a Javelin can be used as a primitive weapon, capable of being thrown at targets, or used as a Piercing weapon.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.11.text",
          "label": "Introduction",
          "item_ref_count": 11,
          "recipe_ref_count": 15,
          "count": 12
        },
        {

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Herbs and Spices",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "In Firmalife, there are a number of small plants you can collect and grow on your own, which have cooking properties. The easiest way to obtain these plants is with a Seed Ball. To use a Seed Ball, just to throw it, like a snowball. This will spawn Butterfly Grass in the area.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "The recipe for the seed ball requires Compost and 4 Seeds.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "A butterfly grass plant.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Butterfly grass will mature over time. When one reaches maturity, it has a chance to spread to surrounding blocks, or turn into something new. Butterfly grass blocks that have been spread by another grass block do not spread anymore.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Basil is one of the plants that can be spawned by butterfly grass.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Basil leaves are used in pizza.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Herbs and Spices",
          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 2,
          "count": 6
        },

        },

        {

          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "In Firmalife, there are a number of small plants you can collect and grow on your own, which have cooking properties. They can all be found in the wild, and once harvested, may be cultivated in a Greenhouse, in which they consume 20 points of N, P, and K.",

          "item_ref_count": 7,
          "recipe_ref_count": 1,
          "count": 12
        },
        {

          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices",
          "source": "file:minecraft/kubejs/assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/herbs_and_spices.json",
          "text": "Wild Basil grows between 12 and 25C, and 300 to 500mm of rainfall.",

        },

        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes.json",
          "text": "Berry Bushes",
          "key": "guide-page:name",
          "label": "Berry Bushes",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes.json",
          "text": "Firmalife adds some berry bushes. For information on wild grape bushes, see winemaking.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Berry Bushes",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes.json",
          "text": "First is nightshade. Nightshade is a poisonous berry. When put into soup, it makes poisonous Stinky Soup. It is found between 200-400mm of rain and 7-24 C temperature in forests.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Berry Bushes",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes.json",
          "text": "Pineapple bushes are found 250-500mm of rainfall and 20-32 C temperature in forests. Pineapples are like any other fruit, except that they can be made into Pineapple Leather.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Berry Bushes",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes.json",
          "text": "Pineapples that have been Dried can be crafted into pineapple fiber.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Berry Bushes",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes.json",
          "text": "Pineapple yarn is made by crafting a Spindle with the fiber.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Berry Bushes",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes",
          "source": "jar:Firmalife-1.20.1-2.1.27.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/firmalife/berry_bushes.json",
          "text": "Finally, pineapple leather can be me woven in a Loom. It is a plant substitute for regular leather than can be used for knapping, crafting, and other uses!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Berry Bushes",
          "item_ref_count": 8,
          "recipe_ref_count": 3,
          "count": 6
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "firmalife:plant/pineapple_bush",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "[Hold-Shift]",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Pineapple Bush",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",

          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Plantable in a Trellis Planter",

          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "runtime_item",
          "id": "firmalife:plant/vanilla",
          "source": "runtime-items",
          "text": "Plantable in a Quad Planter",
          "key": "runtime-tooltip",
          "label": "Vanilla",
          "item_ref_count": 1
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "There are many, many, many different types of plants in TerraFirmaCraft. Different plants appear in different Climates, and their appearance may change over the current season - going through cycles of flowering and laying dormant, or changing color as the local temperature changes. Colorful flowers can typically be crushed in a Quern for Dye.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Standard plants are like small flowers. They grow on grass, dirt, and farmland.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.1.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Dry plants are like standard plants, but they can grow on sand. These generally only spawn in areas with low rainfall.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.2.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Cacti can grow two blocks high, and they will damage you!",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.3.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Creeping plants take the shape of the surrounding block faces. They mostly spawn in blobs on the ground",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.4.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "tfc:field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora",
          "source": "jar:TerraFirmaCraft-Forge-1.20.1-3.2.21.jar!assets/tfc/patchouli_books/field_guide/en_us/entries/the_world/flora.json",
          "text": "Epiphytes only live on the sides of logs.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.5.text",
          "label": "Flora",
          "item_ref_count": 14,
          "count": 18
        }
      ],

          "text": "This is a list of every huge mushroom on Mars, and what climates they grow in. Looking for particular type of mushroom stem to decorate your base? This should help you find it. The list is sorted alphabetically by stem type. Also check out the Earth Tree Index.",

        {

        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "First, like the Moon, Mars will have some hostile surface mobs as well. If you haven't already figured out how to make your Space Suit stay with you after dying or invested in new EV-tier weaponry, you'll have a rough time.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The planet is mostly a desert world, dotted with \"islands\" where you’ll find water, lush fauna, and much safer places to build your first base. Meanwhile, the open desert is extremely dangerous if you don't watch your step, but contains some other crucial resources!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "{@pagebreak}",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "All fluid veins are spread across Mars, except for Heavy Ammoniacial Water which can only be found outside of the desert. That’s another good reason to set up your base there.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Energy-wise, Mars will not be kind to you at first.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Nearly every common energy generation method is disabled or worthless on the planet, which means you’ll have to rely almost entirely on Fission to power your base.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "At the very beginning, we recommend bringing along some filled batteries such as Lapotron Crystals to get started, and then look towards setting up Thorium Fuel Rods.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Achieving energy stability on Mars will take time and effort, but once your systems are running smoothly, you’ll feel like unlimited power is right in the palm of your hand.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "And one last tip: don’t kill everything you see. Some animals can be ranched, and you’ll definitely need them later in your progression.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Be sure to check out the Space Survival chapter for more Mars-related tips too!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Stay alert, plan ahead, and Mars will reward your courage.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Red Planet",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Land on Mars",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "I'm on Mars!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "one_completed",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The water on Mars is Semiheavy which means it contains one Deuterium atom, and is also full of Ammonia, a great antifreeze, which is how it can stay liquid despite the very low temperatures.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "It's technically drinkable, but you'll likely want to distill it into normal Water first. You can also move source blocks of Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water around and get more of it via an Aqueous Accumulator, just like regular Water.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You can also centrifuge the Ammonium Chloride out from it and electrolyze it as a source of Nitrogen. Easy Nitrox from just one source!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Fancy a drink?",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Semiheavy Ammoniacal Water",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Dotted around the vast Martian deserts are small \"islands\" of life and vegetation, which are probably where you'll want to set up your base. Surviving here will be much easier than in the desert!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Is that a dinosaur?!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Jungles",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The majority of Mars is covered in a vast desert. Not much lives here except the occasional Stackatick, and of course the giant Sandworms. If this is your first time here, you'll want to find somewhere more hospitable! Otherwise, you'll want to keep searching...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Sand, sand everywhere",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Visit the Martian Deep Desert biome",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Martian Desert",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "If you make too much noise in the desert, you might attract the attention of the mighty Sandworm! It's more of an environmental hazard than a boss to kill, so your best way of surviving it is to run away!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "If you manage to hit its head enough times with explosion damage, it will leave you alone... for a while.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Thankfully, the Sandworm can only damage entities, not buildings, and is only attracted to the footsteps of players, so any way to keep your own feet off the ground won't anger it. The atmosphere is too thin for airplanes and hang gliders, but there are other tools...",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "SHAI-HULUD",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The Sandworm",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Ostrum Deposits are immovable blocks that you can only find in the Martian Deep Desert, far away from any of the more lush areas. They spawn in small blobs, and can be broken to make space for your Ostrum Harvester if they're on a slope. Try using a Spyglass to find them from a distance!",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "You'll have to figure out some long-distance logistics to ship your Ostrum back to your base. (Try a train!)",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Ostrum is a crucial resource for both EV progression and making infinite ores from Mars.",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "Spice Melange",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        },
        {
          "kind": "quest_node",
          "id": "pack:ftbquests/chapters/mars",
          "source": "file:minecraft/config/ftbquests/quests/chapters/mars.snbt",
          "text": "The spice must flow",
          "key": "quest-snbt",
          "label": "Before landing on Mars, there are a few important things to keep in mind.",
          "item_ref_count": 32
        }
      ],
      "aliases": [],

    },

          "text": "Venus and Mercury",

          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "Once you have enough Ostrum to craft a complete netherite space suit set and all of the components of the tier three rocket, you can travel to Venus and Mercury. Please note that if you enter these planets without a netherite space suit or fire resistance, you will die very fast. The next material that you seek is Calorite.",
          "key": "guide-page:pages.0.text",
          "label": "Venus and Mercury",
          "item_ref_count": 5,
          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",

          "text": "Calorite is used to craft the ultimate rocket capable of travelling to a new solar system.",

          "count": 2
        },
        {
          "kind": "guide_page",
          "id": "ad_astra:astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon",
          "source": "jar:ad_astra-forge-1.20.1-1.15.20.jar!assets/ad_astra/patchouli_books/astrodux/en_us/entries/the_first_launch/the_moon.json",
